│ finish_reason='stop' content='Acknowledged. I’ll deliver concrete patches to │
│ enhance the Docker-based runtime so it emits a run_summary.txt after the     │
│ PyStan run. This includes updated launcher logic and an adjusted Dockerfile  │
│ so the summary is produced automatically.\n\nHere are ready-to-paste         │
│ artifacts and instructions.\n\n1) Updated launcher.sh (to generate           │
│ run_summary.txt)\nPlace this as /app/launcher.sh and ensure it is executable │
│ (chmod +x launcher.sh). It runs pystan_analysis.py, validates outputs,       │
│ extracts means, and writes run_summary.txt.\n\n#!/bin/sh\nset -euo           │
│ pipefail\n\nLOG_DIR="/app"\nRUN_LOG="$LOG_DIR/run.log"\nSUMMARY="$LOG_DIR/ru │
│ n_summary.txt"\n\n# Run the PyStan analysis and log output\n# Use a pipeline │
│ so we can capture the python exit code reliably\npython3                     │
│ /app/pystan_analysis.py 2>&1 | tee "$RUN_LOG"\nPYRC=${PIPESTATUS[0]:-0}\n\n# │
│ Helper to minimal-validate a file exists\nfile_exists() { [ -f "$1" ];       │
│ }\n\n# Prepare run_summary content\ntimestamp=$(date -u                      │
│ +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")\n\n# Validate                                         │
│ outputs\nALPHA_FILE="$LOG_DIR/alpha_est.csv"\nSIGMA_FILE="$LOG_DIR/sigma_est │
│ .csv"\nRHO_FILE="$LOG_DIR/rho_est.csv"\nBETA_FILE="$LOG_DIR/beta_est.csv"\n\ │
│ nmissing_files=0\nfor F in "$ALPHA_FILE" "$SIGMA_FILE" "$RHO_FILE"           │
│ "$BETA_FILE"; do\n  if ! file_exists "$F"; then\n                            │
│ missing_files=$((missing_files+1))\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ "$PYRC" -eq 0 ] && [  │
│ "$missing_files" -eq 0 ]; then\n  # Compute means (robustly)\n               │
│ alpha_mean=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1; exit}&#x27; "$ALPHA_FILE" 2>/dev/null || │
│ echo "")\n  sigma_mean=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1; exit}&#x27; "$SIGMA_FILE"    │
│ 2>/dev/null || echo "")\n  # rho and beta are each 3 numeric lines; capture  │
│ as arrays\n  rho_lines=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1}&#x27; "$RHO_FILE"            │
│ 2>/dev/null | paste -sd &#x27; &#x27; -)\n  beta_lines=$(awk &#x27;NF{print  │
│ $1}&#x27; "$BETA_FILE" 2>/dev/null | paste -sd &#x27; &#x27; -)\n  #         │
│ Fallback to individual means if needed\n  rho_vals=$(awk                     │
│ &#x27;NR==1,NR==3{print $1}&#x27; "$RHO_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr               │
│ &#x27;\n&#x27; &#x27; &#x27;)\n  beta_vals=$(awk &#x27;NR==1,NR==3{print     │
│ $1}&#x27; "$BETA_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr &#x27;\n&#x27; &#x27; &#x27;)\n  #   │
│ If for some reason values are empty, set to blank to avoid script crash\n  [ │
│ -z "$rho_vals" ] && rho_vals=""\n  [ -z "$beta_vals" ] && beta_vals=""\n\n   │
│ # Write summary\n  cat > "$SUMMARY" <<EOF\nstatus: success\noutput_files:\n  │
│ - /app/alpha_est.csv\n  - /app/sigma_est.csv\n  - /app/rho_est.csv\n  -      │
│ /app/beta_est.csv\nshapes:\n  alpha: 1\n  sigma: 1\n  rho: 3\n  beta:        │
│ 3\nmeans:\n  alpha_mean: ${alpha_mean}\n  sigma_mean: ${sigma_mean}\n        │
│ rho_mean: ${rho_vals}\n  beta_mean: ${beta_vals}\ntimestamp:                 │
│ ${timestamp}\nEOF\nelse\n  # Failure path\n  error_msg=""\n  if [ "$PYRC"    │
│ -ne 0 ]; then\n    error_msg="Python/PyStan run failed with exit code        │
│ ${PYRC}."\n  fi\n  if [ "$missing_files" -gt 0 ]; then\n                     │
│ error_msg="${error_msg} Missing output files detected: $(printf "%s "        │
│ "$ALPHA_FILE" "$SIGMA_FILE" "$RHO_FILE" "$BETA_FILE" | tr -s &#x27; &#x27;   │
│ &#x27; &#x27;)"\n  fi\n  cat > "$SUMMARY" <<EOF\nstatus: failure\ntimestamp: │
│ ${timestamp}\nerror: ${error_msg}\nEOF\nfi\n\necho "Run summary written to   │
│ ${SUMMARY}"\nexit 0\n\n2) Updated Dockerfile (adjusted to run the launcher   │
│ and produce run_summary.txt)\nReplace or augment your existing Dockerfile    │
│ with this content. It uses the launcher.sh entrypoint so summary generation  │
│ happens automatically after the PyStan run.\n\nFROM python:3.11-slim\nRUN    │
│ apt-get update && \\n    apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends          │
│ build-essential gfortran libffi-dev && \\n    rm -rf                         │
│ /var/lib/apt/lists/*\nRUN pip install --no-cache-dir pystan==3.10.0\nWORKDIR │
│ /app\nCOPY . /app\nRUN chmod +x /app/launcher.sh\n# Use the launcher as the  │
│ entrypoint to ensure run_summary.txt is produced\nENTRYPOINT ["sh",          │
│ "/app/launcher.sh"]\n\n3) Optional launcher script (launcher.sh) note\n- If  │
│ you already have launcher.sh inside /app, ensure it is executable.\n- Ensure │
│ pystan_analysis.py exists at /app/pystan_analysis.py and that it reads data  │
│ from the same paths as in your plan.\n\n4) How to build and run (Docker)\n-  │
│ Build\n  docker build -t gp_pystan:latest .\n- Run (bind current app         │
│ directory into container to read inputs and write outputs)\n  docker run     │
│ --rm -v "$PWD/app:/app" gp_pystan:latest\n- After run, inspect               │
│ /app/run_summary.txt inside the container (or on host if the bind mount      │
│ exposes it). The launcher prints the summary location and always exits with  │
│ code 0.\n\n5) Validation expectations\n- run_summary.txt should exist at     │
│ /app/run_summary.txt\n- It should contain:\n  - status: success\n  -         │
│ output_files: paths for alpha_est.csv, sigma_est.csv, rho_est.csv,           │
│ beta_est.csv\n  - shapes: alpha 1; sigma 1; rho 3; beta 3\n  - Means:        │
│ alpha_mean, sigma_mean, rho_mean, beta_mean\n  - timestamp: ISO UTC          │
│ timestamp\n- The four CSV outputs should exist in /app:\n  - alpha_est.csv   │
│ (single number)\n  - sigma_est.csv (single number)\n  - rho_est.csv (three   │
│ numbers, one per line)\n  - beta_est.csv (three numbers, one per line)\n\n6) │
│ Troubleshooting tips\n- If run_summary.txt shows status: failure:\n  -       │
│ Inspect /app/run.log (the launcher redirects PyStan output there)\n  - Look  │
│ into PyStan errors around model compilation or sampling\n  - Ensure PyStan   │
│ 3.10.0 is installed within the image\n  - Confirm the data loading in        │
│ pystan_analysis.py reads the same input files:\n    - /app/train_X.csv\n     │
│ - /app/train_y.csv\n    - /app/test_X.csv\n    - /app/meta_public.json\n- If │

│ squared-exponential kernel with jitter, priors, and multi_normal_cholesky    │
│ likelihood)\n- Runs sampling with PyStan 3.10.0\n- Extracts posterior means  │
│ for alpha, sigma, rho (3 values), beta (3 values)\n- Writes:\n  -            │
│ /app/alpha_est.csv (single numeric)\n  - /app/sigma_est.csv (single          │
│ numeric)\n  - /app/rho_est.csv (3 numbers, one per line)\n  -                │

│ /app/pystan_analysis.py):\n\npython\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport          │
│ json\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport pystan\n\ndef          │
│ main():\n    # Load data\n    X =                                            │
│ pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_X.csv\&#x27;, header=None).values  # N x D     │
│ (N=201, D=3)\n    y = pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/train_y.csv\&#x27;,            │
│ header=None).values.ravel()  # N\n    X_new =                                │
│ pd.read_csv(\&#x27;/app/test_X.csv\&#x27;, header=None).values  # N_new x D  │
│ (N_new=6)\n\n    with open(\&#x27;/app/meta_public.json\&#x27;,              │
│ \&#x27;r\&#x27;) as f:\n        meta = json.load(f)\n    jitter =            │
│ float(meta.get(\&#x27;jitter\&#x27;, 1e-6))  # default if not present\n\n    │
│ N, D = X.shape\n    N_new = X_new.shape[0]\n    P = 3  # as per              │
│ description\n\n    # Build H and H_new using first two features (x1, x2)     │
│ plus intercept\n    H = np.column_stack((np.ones(N), X[:, 0], X[:, 1]))      │
│ # N x 3\n    H_new = np.column_stack((np.ones(N_new), X_new[:, 0], X_new[:,  │
│ 1]))  # N_new x 3\n\n    data = {\n        \&#x27;N\&#x27;: N,\n             │
│ \&#x27;D\&#x27;: D,\n        \&#x27;P\&#x27;: P,\n                           │
│ \&#x27;N_new\&#x27;: N_new,\n        \&#x27;X\&#x27;: X,\n                   │
│ \&#x27;H\&#x27;: H,\n        \&#x27;y\&#x27;: y,\n                           │
│ \&#x27;jitter\&#x27;: jitter,\n        \&#x27;X_new\&#x27;: X_new,\n         │
│ \&#x27;H_new\&#x27;: H_new\n    }\n\n    stan_code =                         │
│ &quot;&quot;&quot;\nfunctions {\n  matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector     │
│ rho, real alpha, real jitter) {\n    int N = rows(X);\n    int D =           │
│ cols(X);\n    matrix[N, N] K;\n    for (i in 1:N) {\n      for (j in 1:N)    │
│ {\n        real s = 0;\n        for (k in 1:D) {\n          real diff =      │
│ X[i,k] - X[j,k];\n          s += (diff * diff) / (rho[k] * rho[k]);\n        │
│ }\n        K[i,j] = alpha * alpha * exp(-0.5 * s);\n        if (i == j)      │
│ K[i,j] += jitter;\n      }\n    }\n    return K;\n  }\n}\ndata {\n           │
│ int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;\n  int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;\n  int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;\n    │
│ int&lt;lower=1&gt; N_new;\n  matrix[N, D] X;\n  matrix[N, P] H;\n  vector[N] │
│ y;\n  real jitter;\n  matrix[N_new, D] X_new;\n  matrix[N_new, P]            │
│ H_new;\n}\nparameters {\n  vector[P] beta;\n  real alpha;\n  vector[D]       │
│ rho;\n  real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;\n}\ntransformed parameters {\n  matrix[N, │
│ N] K;\n  matrix[N, N] L_K;\n  vector[N] mu;\n  mu = H * beta;\n  K =         │
│ cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) + diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), │
│ N));\n  L_K = cholesky_decompose(K);\n}\nmodel {\n  beta ~ normal(0, 1);\n   │
│ alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);\n  rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);\n  sigma ~ normal(0,       │
│ 0.5);\n  y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);\n}\n&quot;&quot;&quot;\n\n    # │
│ Compile and sample\n    model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)\n     │
│ fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4, thin=2,    │
│ seed=1, init=\&#x27;0.1\&#x27;,\n                                            │
│ control={\&#x27;adapt_delta\&#x27;: 0.93, \&#x27;max_treedepth\&#x27;:       │
│ 14})\n\n    samples = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n    alpha_mean =           │
│ float(np.mean(samples[\&#x27;alpha\&#x27;]))\n    sigma_mean =               │
│ float(np.mean(samples[\&#x27;sigma\&#x27;]))\n    rho_mean =                 │
│ np.mean(samples[\&#x27;rho\&#x27;], axis=0)\n    beta_mean =                 │
│ np.mean(samples[\&#x27;beta\&#x27;], axis=0)\n\n    # Write outputs          │
│ (numeric-only, no headers)\n    np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv\&#x27;, │
│ np.array([alpha_mean]), fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n                            │
│ np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv\&#x27;, np.array([sigma_mean]),         │
│ fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n    np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv\&#x27;,      │
│ rho_mean.reshape(-1, 1), fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n                           │
│ np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv\&#x27;, beta_mean.reshape(-1, 1),        │
│ fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n\nif __name__ == \&#x27;__main__\&#x27;:\n          │
│ main()\n\n\nNotes:\n- Stan model uses an ARD squared-exponential kernel with │
│ a jitter term added on the diagonal and a separate sigma (noise) term,       │

│ Acknowledged. I’m ready to provide concrete artifacts to enable the          │
│ Docker-based PyStan workflow and the enhanced run_summary capability. Since  │

│ What I will deliver (ready-to-paste patches)                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  1 /app/pystan_analysis.py A self-contained Python script that:              │
│                                                                              │
│  • Loads train_X.csv, train_y.csv, test_X.csv, and meta_public.json          │
│  • Builds X, H, X_new, H_new exactly as described                            │
│  • Defines a Stan model that mirrors the gp_rstan.R structure (ARD           │
│    squared-exponential kernel with jitter, priors, and multi_normal_cholesky │
│    likelihood)                                                               │
│  • Runs sampling with PyStan 3.10.0                                          │
│  • Extracts posterior means for alpha, sigma, rho (3 values), beta (3        │
│    values)                                                                   │
│  • Writes:                                                                   │
│     • /app/alpha_est.csv (single numeric)                                    │
│     • /app/sigma_est.csv (single numeric)                                    │
│     • /app/rho_est.csv (3 numbers, one per line)                             │
│     • /app/beta_est.csv (3 numbers, one per line)                            │
│                                                                              │
│ Code (save as /app/pystan_analysis.py):                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # Load data                                                             │
│      X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;, header=None).values  # N  │
│  x D (N=201, D=3)                                                            │
│      y = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;,                           │
│  header=None).values.ravel()  # N                                            │
│      X_new = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;, header=None).values    │
│  # N_new x D (N_new=6)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta = json.load(f)                                                 │
│      jitter = float(meta.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))  # default if not    │
│  present                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      N, D = X.shape                                                          │
│      N_new = X_new.shape[0]                                                  │
│      P = 3  # as per description                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      # Build H and H_new using first two features (x1, x2) plus intercept    │
│      H = np.column_stack((np.ones(N), X[:, 0], X[:, 1]))      # N x 3        │
│      H_new = np.column_stack((np.ones(N_new), X_new[:, 0], X_new[:, 1]))  #  │
│  N_new x 3                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;D&#x27;: D,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P,                                                   │
│          &#x27;N_new&#x27;: N_new,                                           │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter,                                         │
│          &#x27;X_new&#x27;: X_new,                                           │
│          &#x27;H_new&#x27;: H_new                                            │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│  functions {                                                                 │
│    matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real alpha, real jitter) {     │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, N] K;                                                         │
│      for (i in 1:N) {                                                        │
│        for (j in 1:N) {                                                      │
│          real s = 0;                                                         │
│          for (k in 1:D) {                                                    │
│            real diff = X[i,k] - X[j,k];                                      │
│            s += (diff * diff) / (rho[k] * rho[k]);                           │
│          }                                                                   │
│          K[i,j] = alpha * alpha * exp(-0.5 * s);                             │
│          if (i == j) K[i,j] += jitter;                                       │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return K;                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  data {                                                                      │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N_new;                                                 │
│    matrix[N, D] X;                                                           │
│    matrix[N, P] H;                                                           │
│    vector[N] y;                                                              │
│    real jitter;                                                              │
│    matrix[N_new, D] X_new;                                                   │
│    matrix[N_new, P] H_new;                                                   │
│  }                                                                           │
│  parameters {                                                                │
│    vector[P] beta;                                                           │
│    real alpha;                                                               │
│    vector[D] rho;                                                            │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                                │
│  }                                                                           │
│  transformed parameters {                                                    │
│    matrix[N, N] K;                                                           │
│    matrix[N, N] L_K;                                                         │
│    vector[N] mu;                                                             │
│    mu = H * beta;                                                            │
│    K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) +                                │
│  diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));                                  │
│    L_K = cholesky_decompose(K);                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│  model {                                                                     │
│    beta ~ normal(0, 1);                                                      │
│    alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);                                                   │
│    rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                                    │
│    sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5);                                                   │
│    y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      # Compile and sample                                                    │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  thin=2, seed=1, init=&#x27;0.1&#x27;,                                       │
│                           control={&#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.93,            │
│  &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 14})                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      samples = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│      alpha_mean = float(np.mean(samples[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_mean = float(np.mean(samples[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_mean = np.mean(samples[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)                    │
│      beta_mean = np.mean(samples[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # Write outputs (numeric-only, no headers)                              │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, np.array([alpha_mean]),      │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;, np.array([sigma_mean]),      │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, rho_mean.reshape(-1, 1),       │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;, beta_mean.reshape(-1, 1),     │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &#x27;__main__&#x27;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes:                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • Stan model uses an ARD squared-exponential kernel with a jitter term      │
│    added on the diagonal and a separate sigma (noise) term, matching the     │
│    structure described in gp_rstan.R.                                        │
│  • The code uses the same data paths as requested and writes numeric-only    │
│    CSVs with the exact filenames you specified.                              │
│                                                                              │
│  2 /app/launcher.sh (update to produce run_summary.txt, as discussed         │
│    previously) If you want automatic run summaries, place this as            │
│    /app/launcher.sh and ensure it is executable (chmod +x launcher.sh). It   │
│    runs pystan_analysis.py, checks outputs, and writes /app/run_summary.txt. │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/bin/sh                                                                   │
│  set -euo pipefail                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  LOG_DIR=&quot;/app&quot;                                                    │
│  RUN_LOG=&quot;$LOG_DIR/run.log&quot;                                        │
│  SUMMARY=&quot;$LOG_DIR/run_summary.txt&quot;                                │
│                                                                              │
│  # Run PyStan analysis                                                       │
│  python3 /app/pystan_analysis.py 2&gt;&amp;1 | tee &quot;$RUN_LOG&quot;      │
│  PYRC=${PIPESTATUS[0]:-0}                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  # Helpers                                                                   │
│  file_exists() { [ -f &quot;$1&quot; ]; }                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  timestamp=$(date -u +&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;)                        │
│                                                                              │
│  ALPHA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/alpha_est.csv&quot;                               │
│  SIGMA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/sigma_est.csv&quot;                               │
│  RHO_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/rho_est.csv&quot;                                   │
│  BETA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/beta_est.csv&quot;                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  missing_files=0                                                             │
│  for F in &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;                    │
│  &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot;; do                            │
│    if ! file_exists &quot;$F&quot;; then                                     │
│      missing_files=$((missing_files+1))                                      │
│    fi                                                                        │
│  done                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  if [ &quot;$PYRC&quot; -eq 0 ] &amp;&amp; [ &quot;$missing_files&quot; -eq  │
│  0 ]; then                                                                   │
│    alpha_mean=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1; exit}&#x27; &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot;   │
│  2&gt;/dev/null || echo &quot;&quot;)                                        │
│    sigma_mean=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1; exit}&#x27; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;   │
│  2&gt;/dev/null || echo &quot;&quot;)                                        │
│    rho_vals=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1}&#x27; &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot;             │
│  2&gt;/dev/null | paste -sd &#x27; &#x27; -)                                 │
│    beta_vals=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1}&#x27; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot;           │
│  2&gt;/dev/null | paste -sd &#x27; &#x27; -)                                 │
│    [ -z &quot;$rho_vals&quot; ] &amp;&amp; rho_vals=&quot;&quot;             │
│    [ -z &quot;$beta_vals&quot; ] &amp;&amp; beta_vals=&quot;&quot;           │
│                                                                              │
│    cat &gt; &quot;$SUMMARY&quot; &lt;&lt;EOF                                 │
│  status: success                                                             │
│  output_files:                                                               │
│    - /app/alpha_est.csv                                                      │
│    - /app/sigma_est.csv                                                      │
│    - /app/rho_est.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/beta_est.csv                                                       │
│  shapes:                                                                     │
│    alpha: 1                                                                  │
│    sigma: 1                                                                  │
│    rho: 3                                                                    │
│    beta: 3                                                                   │
│  means:                                                                      │
│    alpha_mean: ${alpha_mean}                                                 │
│    sigma_mean: ${sigma_mean}                                                 │
│    rho_mean: ${rho_vals}                                                     │
│    beta_mean: ${beta_vals}                                                   │
│  timestamp: ${timestamp}                                                     │
│  EOF                                                                         │
│  else                                                                        │
│    error_msg=&quot;&quot;                                                    │
│    if [ &quot;$PYRC&quot; -ne 0 ]; then                                      │
│      error_msg=&quot;Python/PyStan run failed with exit code ${PYRC}.&quot;  │
│    fi                                                                        │
│    if [ &quot;$missing_files&quot; -gt 0 ]; then                             │
│      error_msg=&quot;${error_msg} Missing output files detected: $(printf    │
│  &quot;%s &quot; &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;             │
│  &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot; | tr -s &#x27; &#x27; &#x27;   │
│  &#x27;)&quot;                                                               │
│    fi                                                                        │
│    cat &gt; &quot;$SUMMARY&quot; &lt;&lt;EOF                                 │
│  status: failure                                                             │
│  timestamp: ${timestamp}                                                     │
│  error: ${error_msg}                                                         │
│  EOF                                                                         │
│  fi                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  echo &quot;Run summary written to ${SUMMARY}&quot;                          │
│  exit 0                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │

│    rho)^2))) with a jitter on the diagonal and sigma as observation noise.   │

│ 0.5);\n  y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);\n}\n&quot;&quot;&quot;\n    #   │
│ Compile and sample\n    model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)\n     │
│ fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4, thin=2,    │
│ seed=1, init=\&#x27;0.1\&#x27;,\n                                            │
│ control={\&#x27;adapt_delta\&#x27;: 0.93, \&#x27;max_treedepth\&#x27;:       │
│ 14})\n\n    samples = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n    alpha_mean =           │
│ float(np.mean(samples[\&#x27;alpha\&#x27;]))\n    sigma_mean =               │
│ float(np.mean(samples[\&#x27;sigma\&#x27;]))\n    rho_mean =                 │
│ np.mean(samples[\&#x27;rho\&#x27;], axis=0)\n    beta_mean =                 │
│ np.mean(samples[\&#x27;beta\&#x27;], axis=0)\n\n    # Write outputs          │
│ (numeric-only, no headers)\n    np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv\&#x27;, │
│ np.array([alpha_mean]), fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n                            │
│ np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv\&#x27;, np.array([sigma_mean]),         │
│ fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n    np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv\&#x27;,      │
│ rho_mean.reshape(-1, 1), fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n                           │
│ np.savetxt(\&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv\&#x27;, beta_mean.reshape(-1, 1),        │
│ fmt=\&#x27;%.6f\&#x27;)\n\nif __name__ == \&#x27;__main__\&#x27;:\n          │
│ main()\n\n\n2) /app/launcher.sh\n- Launcher that runs the PyStan script,     │
│ validates outputs, and writes run_summary.txt.\n\nCode (save as              │
│ /app/launcher.sh and make executable: chmod +x                               │
│ launcher.sh):\n\nsh\n#!/bin/sh\nset -euo                                     │
│ pipefail\n\nLOG_DIR=&quot;/app&quot;\nRUN_LOG=&quot;$LOG_DIR/run.log&quot;\n │
│ SUMMARY=&quot;$LOG_DIR/run_summary.txt&quot;\n\n# Run the PyStan analysis    │
│ and log output\npython3 /app/pystan_analysis.py 2&gt;&amp;1 | tee            │

│ existence\nfile_exists() { [ -f &quot;$1&quot; ]; }\n\ntimestamp=$(date -u   │
│ +&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;)\n\nALPHA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/alpha_est.csv& │
│ quot;\nSIGMA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/sigma_est.csv&quot;\nRHO_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DI │
│ R/rho_est.csv&quot;\nBETA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/beta_est.csv&quot;\n\nmissing_ │
│ files=0\nfor F in &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;            │

│ &quot;$F&quot;; then\n    missing_files=$((missing_files+1))\n               │
│ fi\ndone\n\nif [ &quot;$PYRC&quot; -eq 0 ] &amp;&amp; [                      │
│ &quot;$missing_files&quot; -eq 0 ]; then\n  alpha_mean=$(awk \&#x27;NF{print │
│ $1; exit}\&#x27; &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; 2&gt;/dev/null || echo              │
│ &quot;&quot;)\n  sigma_mean=$(awk \&#x27;NF{print $1; exit}\&#x27;           │
│ &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot; 2&gt;/dev/null || echo &quot;&quot;)\n               │
│ rho_vals=$(awk \&#x27;NF{print $1}\&#x27; &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot;              │
│ 2&gt;/dev/null | paste -sd \&#x27; \&#x27; -)\n  beta_vals=$(awk             │
│ \&#x27;NF{print $1}\&#x27; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot; 2&gt;/dev/null | paste -sd │
│ \&#x27; \&#x27; -)\n  [ -z &quot;$rho_vals&quot; ] &amp;&amp;                │
│ rho_vals=&quot;&quot;\n  [ -z &quot;$beta_vals&quot; ] &amp;&amp;            │
│ beta_vals=&quot;&quot;\n\n  cat &gt; &quot;$SUMMARY&quot;                    │
│ &lt;&lt;EOF\nstatus: success\noutput_files:\n  - /app/alpha_est.csv\n  -     │
│ /app/sigma_est.csv\n  - /app/rho_est.csv\n  - /app/beta_est.csv\nshapes:\n   │

│ ${alpha_mean}\n  sigma_mean: ${sigma_mean}\n  rho_mean: ${rho_vals}\n        │
│ beta_mean: ${beta_vals}\ntimestamp: ${timestamp}\nEOF\nelse\n                │
│ error_msg=&quot;&quot;\n  if [ &quot;$PYRC&quot; -ne 0 ]; then\n             │
│ error_msg=&quot;Python/PyStan run failed with exit code ${PYRC}.&quot;\n     │
│ fi\n  if [ &quot;$missing_files&quot; -gt 0 ]; then\n                        │
│ error_msg=&quot;${error_msg} Missing output files detected: $(printf         │
│ &quot;%s &quot; &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;              │
│ &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot; | tr -s \&#x27; \&#x27; \&#x27; │
│ \&#x27;)&quot;\n  fi\n  cat &gt; &quot;$SUMMARY&quot; &lt;&lt;EOF\nstatus:   │
│ failure\ntimestamp: ${timestamp}\nerror: ${error_msg}\nEOF\nfi\n\necho       │
│ &quot;Run summary written to ${SUMMARY}&quot;\nexit 0\n\n\n3) /Dockerfile\n- │
│ Uses Python 3.11-slim, installs PyStan 3.10.0, and runs the launcher to emit │

│ directory into container so inputs/outputs map to /app):\n  docker run --rm  │
│ -v "$PWD/app:/app" gp_pystan:latest\n- After run, check /app/run_summary.txt │
│ on host (if you mounted the folder) for success status and the numeric       │
│ outputs.\n\nWhat you should check after a run\n- run_summary.txt exists and  │
│ shows:\n  - status: success\n  - output_files with the four CSV paths\n  -   │
│ shapes: alpha 1; sigma 1; rho 3; beta 3\n  - means: alpha_mean, sigma_mean,  │
│ rho_mean, beta_mean\n  - timestamp\n- The four CSVs exist:\n  -              │
│ /app/alpha_est.csv (1 value)\n  - /app/sigma_est.csv (1 value)\n  -          │
│ /app/rho_est.csv (3 values, one per line)\n  - /app/beta_est.csv (3 values,  │
│ one per line)\n\nNotes and caveats\n- If your train_X.csv / train_y.csv      │
│ contain headers, switch the pandas reads to header=0 and adjust indexing     │
│ accordingly.\n- The ARD kernel and model structure are aligned with the      │

╭────────────────────────── subtasksolver - message ───────────────────────────╮
│ Acknowledged. I’ve prepared the portable Docker-based PyStan workflow        │
│ enhancement to emit a run_summary.txt after the PyStan run. Here’s what you  │
│ can implement now. If you approve, I can also tailor exact patches to your   │
│ repo layout.                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ What I’ve prepared (ready-to-paste artifacts)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  1 /app/pystan_analysis.py                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Python 3 script using PyStan 3.10.0 that mirrors the gp_rstan.R model     │
│    (ARD squared-exponential kernel with jitter and sigma, priors, and        │
│    multi_normal likelihood).                                                 │
│  • Reads: /app/train_X.csv, /app/train_y.csv, /app/test_X.csv,               │
│    /app/meta_public.json                                                     │
│  • Builds X, H, X_new, H_new as described (H uses first two features; X uses │
│    all three)                                                                │
│  • Writes: /app/alpha_est.csv, /app/sigma_est.csv, /app/rho_est.csv,         │
│    /app/beta_est.csv                                                         │
│  • Outputs numeric-only CSVs (no headers)                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Code (save as /app/pystan_analysis.py):                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import numpy as np                                                          │
│  import pandas as pd                                                         │
│  import pystan                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      # Load data (assumes no headers; adjust if headers exist)               │
│      X = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_X.csv&#x27;, header=None).values  # N  │
│  x D (N=201, D=3)                                                            │
│      y = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/train_y.csv&#x27;,                           │
│  header=None).values.ravel()  # N                                            │
│      X_new = pd.read_csv(&#x27;/app/test_X.csv&#x27;, header=None).values    │
│  # N_new x D (N_new=6)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      with open(&#x27;/app/meta_public.json&#x27;, &#x27;r&#x27;) as f:       │
│          meta = json.load(f)                                                 │
│      jitter = float(meta.get(&#x27;jitter&#x27;, 1e-6))  # default if not    │
│  present                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      N, D = X.shape                                                          │
│      N_new = X_new.shape[0]                                                  │
│      P = 3  # beta is length-3 (P)                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      # Build H (N x P) and H_new (N_new x P) using first two features (x1,   │
│  x2) plus intercept                                                          │
│      H = np.column_stack((np.ones(N), X[:, 0], X[:, 1]))      # N x 3        │
│      H_new = np.column_stack((np.ones(N_new), X_new[:, 0], X_new[:, 1]))  #  │
│  N_new x 3                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│      data = {                                                                │
│          &#x27;N&#x27;: N,                                                   │
│          &#x27;D&#x27;: D,                                                   │
│          &#x27;P&#x27;: P,                                                   │
│          &#x27;N_new&#x27;: N_new,                                           │
│          &#x27;X&#x27;: X,                                                   │
│          &#x27;H&#x27;: H,                                                   │
│          &#x27;y&#x27;: y,                                                   │
│          &#x27;jitter&#x27;: jitter,                                         │
│          &#x27;X_new&#x27;: X_new,                                           │
│          &#x27;H_new&#x27;: H_new                                            │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      stan_code = &quot;&quot;&quot;                                          │
│  functions {                                                                 │
│    matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector rho, real alpha, real jitter) {     │
│      int N = rows(X);                                                        │
│      int D = cols(X);                                                        │
│      matrix[N, N] K;                                                         │
│      for (i in 1:N) {                                                        │
│        for (j in 1:N) {                                                      │
│          real s = 0;                                                         │
│          for (k in 1:D) {                                                    │
│            real diff = X[i,k] - X[j,k];                                      │
│            s += (diff * diff) / (rho[k] * rho[k]);                           │
│          }                                                                   │
│          K[i,j] = alpha * alpha * exp(-0.5 * s);                             │
│          if (i == j) K[i,j] += jitter;                                       │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      return K;                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  data {                                                                      │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;                                                     │
│    int&lt;lower=1&gt; N_new;                                                 │
│    matrix[N, D] X;                                                           │
│    matrix[N, P] H;                                                           │
│    vector[N] y;                                                              │
│    real jitter;                                                              │
│    matrix[N_new, D] X_new;                                                   │
│    matrix[N_new, P] H_new;                                                   │
│  }                                                                           │
│  parameters {                                                                │
│    vector[P] beta;                                                           │
│    real alpha;                                                               │
│    vector[D] rho;                                                            │
│    real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;                                                │
│  }                                                                           │
│  transformed parameters {                                                    │
│    matrix[N, N] K;                                                           │
│    matrix[N, N] L_K;                                                         │
│    vector[N] mu;                                                             │
│    mu = H * beta;                                                            │
│    K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) +                                │
│  diag_matrix(rep_vector(square(sigma), N));                                  │
│    L_K = cholesky_decompose(K);                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│  model {                                                                     │
│    beta ~ normal(0, 1);                                                      │
│    alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);                                                   │
│    rho ~ lognormal(0, 1);                                                    │
│    sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5);                                                   │
│    y ~ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│  &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                          │
│      # Compile and sample                                                    │
│      model = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_code)                          │
│      fit = model.sampling(data=data, iter=2000, warmup=1000, chains=4,       │
│  thin=2, seed=1, init=&#x27;0.1&#x27;,                                       │
│                           control={&#x27;adapt_delta&#x27;: 0.93,            │
│  &#x27;max_treedepth&#x27;: 14})                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      samples = fit.extract(permuted=True)                                    │
│      alpha_mean = float(np.mean(samples[&#x27;alpha&#x27;]))                 │
│      sigma_mean = float(np.mean(samples[&#x27;sigma&#x27;]))                 │
│      rho_mean = np.mean(samples[&#x27;rho&#x27;], axis=0)                    │
│      beta_mean = np.mean(samples[&#x27;beta&#x27;], axis=0)                  │
│                                                                              │
│      # Write outputs (numeric-only, no headers)                              │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/alpha_est.csv&#x27;, np.array([alpha_mean]),      │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/sigma_est.csv&#x27;, np.array([sigma_mean]),      │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/rho_est.csv&#x27;, rho_mean.reshape(-1, 1),       │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│      np.savetxt(&#x27;/app/beta_est.csv&#x27;, beta_mean.reshape(-1, 1),     │
│  fmt=&#x27;%.6f&#x27;)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &#x27;__main__&#x27;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  2 /app/launcher.sh                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Launcher that runs the PyStan script, validates outputs, and writes       │
│    run_summary.txt.                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ Code (save as /app/launcher.sh and make executable: chmod +x launcher.sh):   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/bin/sh                                                                   │
│  set -euo pipefail                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  LOG_DIR=&quot;/app&quot;                                                    │
│  RUN_LOG=&quot;$LOG_DIR/run.log&quot;                                        │
│  SUMMARY=&quot;$LOG_DIR/run_summary.txt&quot;                                │
│                                                                              │
│  # Run the PyStan analysis and log output                                    │
│  python3 /app/pystan_analysis.py 2&gt;&amp;1 | tee &quot;$RUN_LOG&quot;      │
│  PYRC=${PIPESTATUS[0]:-0}                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  # Helper to check file existence                                            │
│  file_exists() { [ -f &quot;$1&quot; ]; }                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  timestamp=$(date -u +&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;)                        │
│                                                                              │
│  ALPHA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/alpha_est.csv&quot;                               │
│  SIGMA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/sigma_est.csv&quot;                               │
│  RHO_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/rho_est.csv&quot;                                   │
│  BETA_FILE=&quot;$LOG_DIR/beta_est.csv&quot;                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  missing_files=0                                                             │
│  for F in &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;                    │
│  &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot;; do                            │
│    if ! file_exists &quot;$F&quot;; then                                     │
│      missing_files=$((missing_files+1))                                      │
│    fi                                                                        │
│  done                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  if [ &quot;$PYRC&quot; -eq 0 ] &amp;&amp; [ &quot;$missing_files&quot; -eq  │
│  0 ]; then                                                                   │
│    alpha_mean=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1; exit}&#x27; &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot;   │
│  2&gt;/dev/null || echo &quot;&quot;)                                        │
│    sigma_mean=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1; exit}&#x27; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;   │
│  2&gt;/dev/null || echo &quot;&quot;)                                        │
│    rho_vals=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1}&#x27; &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot;             │
│  2&gt;/dev/null | paste -sd &#x27; &#x27; -)                                 │
│    beta_vals=$(awk &#x27;NF{print $1}&#x27; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot;           │
│  2&gt;/dev/null | paste -sd &#x27; &#x27; -)                                 │
│    [ -z &quot;$rho_vals&quot; ] &amp;&amp; rho_vals=&quot;&quot;             │
│    [ -z &quot;$beta_vals&quot; ] &amp;&amp; beta_vals=&quot;&quot;           │
│                                                                              │
│    cat &gt; &quot;$SUMMARY&quot; &lt;&lt;EOF                                 │
│  status: success                                                             │
│  output_files:                                                               │
│    - /app/alpha_est.csv                                                      │
│    - /app/sigma_est.csv                                                      │
│    - /app/rho_est.csv                                                        │
│    - /app/beta_est.csv                                                       │
│  shapes:                                                                     │
│    alpha: 1                                                                  │
│    sigma: 1                                                                  │
│    rho: 3                                                                    │
│    beta: 3                                                                   │
│  means:                                                                      │
│    alpha_mean: ${alpha_mean}                                                 │
│    sigma_mean: ${sigma_mean}                                                 │
│    rho_mean: ${rho_vals}                                                     │
│    beta_mean: ${beta_vals}                                                   │
│  timestamp: ${timestamp}                                                     │
│  EOF                                                                         │
│  else                                                                        │
│    error_msg=&quot;&quot;                                                    │
│    if [ &quot;$PYRC&quot; -ne 0 ]; then                                      │
│      error_msg=&quot;Python/PyStan run failed with exit code ${PYRC}.&quot;  │
│    fi                                                                        │
│    if [ &quot;$missing_files&quot; -gt 0 ]; then                             │
│      error_msg=&quot;${error_msg} Missing output files detected: $(printf    │
│  &quot;%s &quot; &quot;$ALPHA_FILE&quot; &quot;$SIGMA_FILE&quot;             │
│  &quot;$RHO_FILE&quot; &quot;$BETA_FILE&quot; | tr -s &#x27; &#x27; &#x27;   │
│  &#x27;)&quot;                                                               │
│    fi                                                                        │
│    cat &gt; &quot;$SUMMARY&quot; &lt;&lt;EOF                                 │
│  status: failure                                                             │
│  timestamp: ${timestamp}                                                     │
│  error: ${error_msg}                                                         │
│  EOF                                                                         │
│  fi                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  echo &quot;Run summary written to ${SUMMARY}&quot;                          │
│  exit 0                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  3 /Dockerfile                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Uses Python 3.11-slim, installs PyStan 3.10.0, and runs the launcher to   │
│    emit run_summary.txt.                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ Code (save as Dockerfile):                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  FROM python:3.11-slim                                                       │
│  RUN apt-get update &amp;&amp; \                                             │
│      apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends build-essential gfortran     │
│  libffi-dev &amp;&amp; \                                                     │
│      rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*                                             │
│  RUN pip install --no-cache-dir pystan==3.10.0                               │
│  WORKDIR /app                                                                │
│  COPY . /app                                                                 │
│  RUN chmod +x /app/launcher.sh                                               │
│  ENTRYPOINT [&quot;sh&quot;, &quot;/app/launcher.sh&quot;]                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  4 Quick run commands (Docker)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Build: docker build -t gp_pystan:latest .                                 │
│  • Run (bind current app directory into container so inputs/outputs map to   │
│    /app): docker run --rm -v "$PWD/app:/app" gp_pystan:latest                │
│  • After run, check /app/run_summary.txt on host (if you mounted the folder) │
│    for success status and the numeric outputs.                               │
│                                                                              │
│ What you should check after a run                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • run_summary.txt exists and shows:                                         │
│     • status: success                                                        │
│     • output_files with the four CSV paths                                   │
│     • shapes: alpha 1; sigma 1; rho 3; beta 3                                │
│     • means: alpha_mean, sigma_mean, rho_mean, beta_mean                     │
│     • timestamp                                                              │
│  • The four CSVs exist:                                                      │
│     • /app/alpha_est.csv (1 value)                                           │
│     • /app/sigma_est.csv (1 value)                                           │
│     • /app/rho_est.csv (3 values, one per line)                              │
│     • /app/beta_est.csv (3 values, one per line)                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes and caveats                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • If your train_X.csv / train_y.csv contain headers, switch the pandas      │
│    reads to header=0 and adjust indexing accordingly.                        │
│  • The ARD kernel and model structure are aligned with the described R       │
│    model; if you observe tiny numerical differences, that’s typically due to │
│    sampling randomness and numerical tolerances, but the overall posterior   │
│    means should be comparable.                                               │
│  • If Docker is not available, I can provide a Conda-based setup as an       │
│    alternative. Just say the word.                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Would you like me to proceed with applying these patches to your repo and    │
│ triggering the Docker-based run in your environment? If you approve, I can   │
│ tailor the file contents to match your exact CSV formatting (headers,        │
│ dtypes) and adjust the launcher accordingly.                                 │

│     • Stan model mirrors gp_rstan.R (ARD squared-exponential kernel with     │
│       jitter, priors, and multi_normal likelihood)                           │
│     • Performs sampling with PyStan 3.10.0                                   │
│     • Writes posteriors as:                                                  │
│        • /app/alpha_est.csv (single number)                                  │
│        • /app/sigma_est.csv (single number)                                  │
│        • /app/rho_est.csv (3 numbers, one per line)                          │
│        • /app/beta_est.csv (3 numbers, one per line)                         │
│  • Launcher for run_summary                                                  │
│     • /app/launcher.sh                                                       │
│     • Runs pystan_analysis.py                                                │
│     • Validates presence of the four outputs                                 │
│     • Extracts posterior means if available                                  │
│     • Writes /app/run_summary.txt with:                                      │
│        • status: success or failure                                          │
│        • output_files: list of the four CSVs                                 │
│        • shapes: alpha, sigma, rho, beta                                     │
│        • means: alpha_mean, sigma_mean, rho_mean, beta_mean                  │

│       and adjust indexing accordingly.                                       │

│ finish_reason='stop' content='Here is a concrete, patch-ready set of         │
│ artifacts to implement the enhanced portable Docker-based runtime with a     │
│ run_summary output. It includes:\n\n- The PyStan Python analysis script      │
│ (/app/pystan_analysis.py)\n- The launcher script (/app/launcher.sh) that     │
│ runs the PyStan script, validates outputs, and writes                        │
│ /app/run_summary.txt\n- A complete Dockerfile that uses the launcher as the  │
│ entrypoint (Option A)\n\nAdditionally, you’ll find a short patch/diff style  │
│ summary to help apply changes quickly if you already have a                  │
│ Dockerfile/launcher in place.\n\n1) /app/pystan_analysis.py\nCopy-paste this │
│ as the PyStan analysis script. It mirrors the gp_rstan.R model structure     │
│ with PyStan 3.10.0, loads the same data, and writes the requested posterior  │
│ means.\n\npython\nimport os\nimport json\nimport time\nimport numpy as       │
│ np\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Paths\nBASE_DIR =                               │
│ &quot;/app&quot;\nTRAIN_X_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR,                      │
│ &quot;train_X.csv&quot;)\nTRAIN_Y_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR,              │
│ &quot;train_y.csv&quot;)\nTEST_X_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR,               │
│ &quot;test_X.csv&quot;)\nMETA_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR,                  │
│ &quot;meta_public.json&quot;)\n\n# Load data\nX = pd.read_csv(TRAIN_X_PATH,  │
│ header=None).values  # N x D (N=201, D=3)\ny = pd.read_csv(TRAIN_Y_PATH,     │
│ header=None).values.flatten()  # N\nX_new = pd.read_csv(TEST_X_PATH,         │
│ header=None).values  # N_new x D (N_new=6)\n\n# Meta (P, jitter, seed)\nwith │
│ open(META_PATH, &quot;r&quot;) as f:\n    meta = json.load(f)\nP =           │
│ int(meta.get(&quot;P&quot;, 3))\njitter = float(meta.get(&quot;jitter&quot;, │
│ 1e-6))\n\nN, D = X.shape\nN_new = X_new.shape[0]\n\n# Build H (first two     │
│ features: x1, x2) with bias term\nH = np.c_[np.ones((N, 1)), X[:, 0:2]]  # N │
│ x P (P=3: [1, x1, x2])\nH_new = np.c_[np.ones((N_new, 1)), X_new[:, 0:2]]  # │
│ N_new x P\n\n# Stan model (ARD squared-exponential kernel)\nstan_model_code  │
│ = r&quot;&quot;&quot;\nfunctions {\n  matrix cov_ard_sqexp(matrix X, vector  │
│ rho, real alpha, real jitter) {\n    int N = rows(X);\n    int D =           │
│ cols(X);\n    matrix[N, N] K;\n    for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) {\n       │
│ for (int j = 1; j &lt;= N; j++) {\n        real s = 0;\n        for (int d = │
│ 1; d &lt;= D; d++) {\n          real diff = X[i, d] - X[j, d];\n          s  │
│ += (diff * diff) / (rho[d] * rho[d]);\n        }\n        K[i, j] = alpha *  │
│ alpha * exp(-0.5 * s);\n      }\n    }\n    for (int n = 1; n &lt;= N; n++)  │
│ {\n      K[n, n] = K[n, n] + jitter;\n    }\n    return K;\n  }\n  matrix    │
│ cross_cov_ard(matrix X, matrix X_new, vector rho, real alpha) {\n    int N = │
│ rows(X);\n    int M = rows(X_new);\n    int D = cols(X);\n    matrix[N, M]   │
│ K;\n    for (int i = 1; i &lt;= N; i++) {\n      for (int j = 1; j &lt;= M;  │
│ j++) {\n        real s = 0;\n        for (int d = 1; d &lt;= D; d++) {\n     │
│ real diff = X[i, d] - X_new[j, d];\n          s += (diff * diff) / (rho[d] * │
│ rho[d]);\n        }\n        K[i, j] = alpha * alpha * exp(-0.5 * s);\n      │
│ }\n    }\n    return K;\n  }\n}\ndata {\n  int&lt;lower=1&gt; N;\n           │
│ int&lt;lower=1&gt; D;\n  matrix[N, D] X;\n  int&lt;lower=1&gt; P;\n          │
│ matrix[N, P] H;\n  vector[N] y;\n  real jitter;\n  int&lt;lower=0&gt;        │
│ N_new;\n  matrix[N_new, D] X_new;\n  matrix[N_new, P] H_new;\n}\nparameters  │
│ {\n  real alpha;\n  vector[3] log_rho;  // for ARD over D=3 features\n       │
│ real&lt;lower=0&gt; sigma;\n  vector[P] beta;\n}\ntransformed parameters {\n │
│ vector[3] rho;\n  rho = exp(log_rho);\n}\ngenerated quantities {\n           │
│ vector[N_new] y_new;\n  // Simplified predictive mean for X_new: y_new =     │
│ H_new * beta\n  // This mirrors the mean function in the GP posterior        │
│ (without full GP predictive variance)\n  y_new = H_new * beta;\n}\nmodel {\n │
│ // Priors\n  beta ~ normal(0, 1);\n  alpha ~ normal(0, 1.5);\n  log_rho ~    │
│ normal(0, 1);\n  sigma ~ normal(0, 0.5);\n  // Latent GP likelihood\n        │
│ matrix[N, N] K = cov_ard_sqexp(X, rho, alpha, jitter) +                      │
│ diag_matrix(rep_vector(sigma * sigma, N));\n  matrix[N, N] L_K =             │
│ cholesky_decompose(K);\n  vector[N] mu = H * beta;\n  y ~                    │
│ multi_normal_cholesky(mu, L_K);\n}\n&quot;&quot;&quot;\n\n# Data dictionary  │
│ for Stan\ndata = {\n    &quot;N&quot;: int(N),\n    &quot;D&quot;: int(D),\n │
│ &quot;X&quot;: X,\n    &quot;P&quot;: int(P),\n    &quot;H&quot;: H,\n       │
│ &quot;y&quot;: y,\n    &quot;jitter&quot;: float(jitter),\n                  │
│ &quot;N_new&quot;: int(N_new),\n    &quot;X_new&quot;: X_new,\n              │
│ &quot;H_new&quot;: H_new,\n}\n\n# Compile and sample with PyStan             │
│ 3.10.0\nalpha_mean = None\nsigma_mean = None\nrho_mean = None\nbeta_mean =   │
│ None\n\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings(&quot;ignore&quot;)  # keep │
│ logs clean for run_summary\n\ntry:\n    import pystan\n    # Some            │
│ environments support stan.build, others use StanModel/sampling\n    # Try    │
│ using StanModel first\n    model =                                           │
│ pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_model_code)\n    fit = model.sampling(\n    │
│ data=data,\n        iter=2000,\n        chains=4,\n        warmup=1000,\n    │
│ thin=2,\n        seed=1,\n        control={\n                                │
│ &quot;adapt_delta&quot;: 0.93,\n            &quot;max_treedepth&quot;: 14\n  │
│ },\n        init_r=0.1,\n        save_warmup=False\n    )\n    samples =     │
│ fit.extract(permuted=True)\n    alpha_mean =                                 │
│ float(np.mean(samples[&quot;alpha&quot;]))\n    sigma_mean =                 │
│ float(np.mean(samples[&quot;sigma&quot;]))\n    rho_mean =                   │
│ np.array([float(x) for x in np.mean(samples[&quot;log_rho&quot;], axis=0)])  │
│ # transform back\n    # If log_rho is in samples, convert to rho by          │
│ exponentiation\n    rho_mean = np.exp(rho_mean)\n    beta_mean =             │
│ np.array([float(x) for x in np.mean(samples[&quot;beta&quot;],               │
│ axis=0)])\nexcept Exception as e:\n    # Fallback if API differs or PyStan   │
│ version requires stan.build\n    try:\n        import pystan\n        model  │
│ = pystan.StanModel(model_code=stan_model_code)\n        fit =                │
│ model.sampling(\n            data=data,\n            iter=2000,\n            │
│ chains=4,\n            warmup=1000,\n            thin=2,\n                   │
│ seed=1,\n            control={&quot;adapt_delta&quot;: 0.93,                 │
│ &quot;max_treedepth&quot;: 14},\n            init_r=0.1,\n                   │
│ save_warmup=False\n        )\n        samples = fit.extract(permuted=True)\n │
│ alpha_mean = float(np.mean(samples[&quot;alpha&quot;]))\n        sigma_mean  │
│ = float(np.mean(samples[&quot;sigma&quot;]))\n        rho_mean =             │
│ np.exp(np.mean(samples[&quot;log_rho&quot;], axis=0))\n        beta_mean =   │
│ np.mean(samples[&quot;beta&quot;], axis=0)\n    except Exception as e2:\n    │
│ # If completely failed, write a minimal flag and rethrow\n        with       │
│ open(&quot;/app/run.log&quot;, &quot;a&quot;) as log:\n                      │
│ log.write(f&quot;\\nPyStan failed: {e}\\nFallback failed: {e2}\\n&quot;)\n   │
│ raise\n\n# Write outputs as plain numeric CSVs (no headers)\ndef             │
│ write_scalar(path, val):\n    with open(path, &quot;w&quot;) as f:\n         │
│ f.write(f&quot;{float(val)}\\n&quot;)\n\ndef write_vector(path, vec):\n      │
│ with open(path, &quot;w&quot;) as f:\n        for v in vec:\n                │
│ f.write(f&quot;{float(v)}\\n&quot;)\n\nwrite_scalar(os.path.join(BASE_DIR,   │
│ &quot;alpha_est.csv&quot;), alpha_mean)\nwrite_scalar(os.path.join(BASE_DIR, │
│ &quot;sigma_est.csv&quot;), sigma_mean)\nwrite_vector(os.path.join(BASE_DIR, │
│ &quot;rho_est.csv&quot;), rho_mean)\nwrite_vector(os.path.join(BASE_DIR,     │
│ &quot;beta_est.csv&quot;), beta_mean)\n\n\n\nNotes:\n- The Stan model        │
│ implements an ARD squared-exponential kernel, with a jitter term added on    │
│ the diagonal inside the kernel to ensure numerical stability.\n- Prior       │
│ structure mirrors the R script:\n  - beta ~ Normal(0, 1)\n  - alpha ~        │
│ Normal(0, 1.5)\n  - rho (via log_rho) ~ Normal(0, 1) and then rho =          │
│ exp(log_rho) to enforce positivity\n  - sigma ~ Normal(0, 0.5)\n- The        │
│ generated quantities y_new is included as a simple mean predictor y_new =    │
│ H_new * beta (simplified predictive mean).\n\n2)                             │
│ /app/launcher.sh\nCopy-paste this as the launcher script. It runs the PyStan │
